=== focus.ts ADDITIONS (additive only, do not modify existing code) ===

IMPORTANT: focus.ts must remain pure — NO direct Node.js imports (no 'fs', no 'path', no 'events'). All filesystem operations go through the injected FocusFs interface.

--- FocusFs interface ---

```typescript
export interface FocusFs {
  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
  watchDir(
    dir: string,
    onChange: () => void,
    onError: (err: unknown) => void
  ): { close(): void };
}
```

- `readFile`: reads file contents as string, returns null if file missing or unreadable
- `watchDir`: watches a directory for changes, calls onChange on any change, onError on watcher error. Returns a handle with close().

--- watchFocusFile ---

```typescript
export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
  dir: string;
  filename: string;
  fs: FocusFs;
  workspaceId: string;
  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
}

export function watchFocusFile(
  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
): { dispose(): void }
```

Behavior:
1. On creation, read the file at `${dir}/${filename}`. If it exists and parses successfully, call `markSeen(request.requestId)` on an internal seen-set (do NOT fire onRequest — this is startup suppression). The internal seen-set is a simple Set<string> or array, separate from FocusQueue.
2. Start watching the directory via `fs.watchDir(dir, onChange, onError)`.
3. On change: read the file, parse with parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {workspaceId}). If ok and requestId not in the seen-set, fire onRequest(request) and add requestId to seen-set.
4. On error: close the current watcher and re-attach after a delay. Use a timer reference (the timer should be unreferenced so it doesn't keep the process alive — note this in a comment since the caller is responsible for unref'ing in the vscode layer).
5. On dispose: close the watcher, clear any pending re-attach timer.
6. The re-attach delay should be configurable or defaulted (e.g. 1000ms).
7. Guard against double-fire from rapid fs events: use a debounce or a simple "reading" flag so concurrent change events don't trigger multiple reads.

Implementation notes:
- Keep the seen-set unbounded for now — the file watcher lifetime matches the extension lifecycle, so it won't grow indefinitely.
- Parse the file content as JSON. If JSON.parse throws, silently ignore (corrupted/partial write).
- The path join `${dir}/${filename}` is simple string concatenation — no Node path module needed.

```typescript
function mockFs(content: string | null): FocusFs & { triggerChange: () => void; triggerError: (err: unknown) => void } {
  let onChange: (() => void) | undefined;
  let onError: ((err: unknown) => void) | undefined;
  return {
    readFile: async () => content,
    watchDir: (dir, onC, onE) => {
      onChange = onC;
      onError = onE;
      return { close: vi.fn() };
    },
    triggerChange: () => onChange?.(),
    triggerError: (err) => onError?.(err),
  };
}
```

# Adapter: Software

Load this adapter only when the user explicitly asks for code, debugging, implementation, tests, APIs, data pipelines, executable notebooks, build output, or changes inside a codebase.

## Vocabulary Mapping

Software tasks may use these terms because they match the domain:

| Software term | Generic equivalent |
|---|---|
| Object or class | Artifact with state and behavior. |
| Interface | Handoff contract or public boundary. |
| Test | Deterministic acceptance check. |
| Refactor | Improve structure while preserving behavior. |
| Module | Artifact group with one responsibility. |
| Service | Component that owns one capability. |

## Procedure

1. Read the relevant files before editing.
2. Identify the smallest behavior-preserving or feature-complete change.
3. Prefer existing project patterns over new abstractions.
4. Add or update tests when the change affects behavior.
5. Run the narrowest useful validation first.
6. Report changed paths and validation results.

## Checks

- The change is scoped to the requested behavior.
- Existing behavior is preserved unless the user requested otherwise.
- Tests or equivalent checks were run, or the reason they could not run is reported.
- No unrelated files were reformatted or rewritten.

// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
import { isNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with { 'resolution-mode': 'import' };
import type { FocusRequest } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with { 'resolution-mode': 'import' };

export type FocusFilePayload =
  | { ok: true; request: FocusRequest }
  | { ok: false; reason: FocusParseError };

export type FocusParseError =
  | 'invalid_shape'
  | 'wrong_workspace'
  | 'invalid_node_id'
  | 'missing_request_id';

function hasOwn(value: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): boolean {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key);
}

export function parseFocusFilePayload(
  input: unknown,
  expected: { workspaceId: string }
): FocusFilePayload {
  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'invalid_shape' };
  }

  const payload = input as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (
    typeof payload.workspace !== 'string' ||
    typeof payload.requestId !== 'string' ||
    typeof payload.nodeId !== 'string'
  ) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'invalid_shape' };
  }

  if (payload.workspace !== expected.workspaceId) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'wrong_workspace' };
  }

  if (payload.requestId.length === 0) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'missing_request_id' };
  }

  if (!isNodeId(payload.nodeId)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'invalid_node_id' };
  }

  if (hasOwn(payload, 'center') && typeof payload.center !== 'boolean') {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'invalid_shape' };
  }

  const request: FocusRequest = {
    requestId: payload.requestId,
    nodeId: payload.nodeId as NodeId,
  };
  if (hasOwn(payload, 'center')) {
    request.center = payload.center as boolean;
  }

  return { ok: true, request };
}

export class FocusQueue {
  private readonly maxSeen: number;
  private queue: FocusRequest[] = [];
  private ready = false;
  private seen: string[] = [];

  constructor(options?: { maxSeen?: number }) {
    const maxSeen = options?.maxSeen ?? 1000;
    this.maxSeen =
      typeof maxSeen === 'number' && Number.isFinite(maxSeen)
        ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxSeen))
        : 1000;
  }

  enqueue(request: FocusRequest): void {
    if (
      this.seen.includes(request.requestId) ||
      this.queue.some(item => item.requestId === request.requestId)
    ) {
      return;
    }

    if (this.ready) {
      this.queue.push(request);
    } else {
      this.queue = [request];
    }
  }

  markReady(): void {
    this.ready = true;
  }

  markNotReady(): void {
    this.ready = false;
    if (this.queue.length > 1) {
      this.queue = [this.queue[this.queue.length - 1]];
    }
  }

  drain(): FocusRequest[] {
    const drained = this.queue;
    this.queue = [];
    for (const request of drained) {
      this.markSeen(request.requestId);
    }
    return drained;
  }

  markSeen(requestId: string): void {
    if (this.seen.includes(requestId)) {
      return;
    }

    this.seen.push(requestId);
    if (this.seen.length > this.maxSeen) {
      this.seen = this.seen.slice(Math.floor(this.seen.length / 2));
    }
  }

  get pending(): number {
    return this.queue.length;
  }
}

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with { 'resolution-mode': 'import' };
import { FocusQueue, parseFocusFilePayload } from '../focus';

const nodeId = 'vault:test-node' as NodeId;

function request(requestId: string) {
  return { requestId, nodeId };
}

describe('focus', () => {
  it('test_focus_parse_valid', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      request: { requestId: 'request-1', nodeId },
    });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_valid_center', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        {
          workspace: 'workspace-a',
          requestId: 'request-1',
          nodeId,
          center: true,
        },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      request: { requestId: 'request-1', nodeId, center: true },
    });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_invalid_shape', () => {
    for (const input of [
      null,
      42,
      {},
      { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1' },
      { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId: 42 },
    ]) {
      expect(
        parseFocusFilePayload(input, { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' })
      ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'invalid_shape' });
    }
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_wrong_workspace', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-b', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'wrong_workspace' });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_missing_request_id', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: '', nodeId },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'missing_request_id' });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_invalid_node_id', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId: 'bad' },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'invalid_node_id' });
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_replay_suppression', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue({ requestId: 'request-1', nodeId: 'vault:other-node' as NodeId });

    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-1')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_keep_latest_not_ready', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue(request('request-2'));

    expect(queue.pending).toBe(1);
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-2')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_accumulate_ready', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.markReady();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue(request('request-2'));

    expect(queue.pending).toBe(2);
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-1'), request('request-2')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_mark_not_ready_keeps_latest', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.markReady();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue(request('request-2'));
    queue.markNotReady();

    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-2')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_mark_seen_suppresses', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.markSeen('request-1');
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));

    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_drain_adds_to_seen', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-1')]);

    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([]);
  });
});

import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
import path from 'path';
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';

const __dirname = fileURLToPath(new URL('.', import.meta.url));

function findSpecFiles(dir: string, base = dir): string[] {
  const results: string[] = [];
  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
    const full = path.join(dir, entry);
    if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) {
      results.push(...findSpecFiles(full, base));
    } else if (entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) {
      results.push(path.relative(base, full));
    }
  }
  return results;
}

function getUnitReadySpecFiles(): string[] {
  const srcDir = path.join(__dirname, 'src');
  return findSpecFiles(srcDir)
    .filter(file => {
      const content = readFileSync(path.join(srcDir, file), 'utf8');
      return (
        content.includes('/* @unit-ready */') ||
        content.includes('// @unit-ready')
      );
    })
    .map(file => `src/${file}`);
}

const excludeSpecs = process.env.EXCLUDE_SPECS === 'true';
const unitReadySpecs = excludeSpecs ? [] : getUnitReadySpecFiles();

  resolve: {
    alias: {
      vscode: path.join(__dirname, 'src/test/vscode-mock.ts'),
      // Resolve @foam/core to TS source under vitest so the test-utils
      // subpath export shares module identity with the main entry.
      // Without this, `@foam/core` resolves to compiled out/ while
      // `@foam/core/test` (which relative-imports ../src/...) resolves
      // to source — two instances of URI etc., breaking `instanceof`.
      // Order matters: longer prefix first.
      '@foam/core/test': path.join(__dirname, '../foam-core/test/test-utils.ts'),
      '@foam/core/scripting': path.join(
        __dirname,
        '../foam-core/src/scripting/index.ts'
      ),
      '@foam/core': path.join(__dirname, '../foam-core/src/index.ts'),
      '@vscode/extension-telemetry': path.join(
        __dirname,
        'src/test/extension-telemetry-mock.ts'
      ),
    },
  },
  // Mirror the version constants injected by esbuild at build time so
  // source files that read them (e.g. extension.ts for telemetry) don't
  // hit a runtime ReferenceError in tests. Literal sentinel values keep
  // tests independent of the actual package versions.

if [ "$TSC_ERRORS" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL k_N12b_2 tsc errors=$TSC_ERRORS"
  echo "$TSC_OUT" | head -5
  FAILS=$((FAILS+1))
fi

LINT_OUT=$(cd "$PKG" && ../../node_modules/.bin/oxlint src/pa-hub/focus.ts 2>&1 || true)

if [ "$LINT_ERR" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL k_N12b_2 oxlint errors=$LINT_ERR"
  FAILS=$((FAILS+1))
fi

# 4. regression gates
for k in privacy roots graph_data graph_view open_node privacy_index; do
  GATE_OUT=$(cd "$FX" && python3 scripts/pa_hub_gate.py -k "$k" 2>&1 | /usr/bin/grep '^PASS\|^FAIL' || echo "ERROR $k")
  if echo "$GATE_OUT" | /usr/bin/grep -q '^FAIL\|^ERROR'; then
    echo "FAIL k_N12b_2 regression gate $k: $GATE_OUT"
    FAILS=$((FAILS+1))
  fi
done

if [ "$FAILS" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL k_N12b_2 total_failures=$FAILS"
  exit 1
fi
echo "PASS k_N12b_2 focus=$PASS_CT tsc=0 oxlint=$LINT_ERR regressions=ok"

+
+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+function mockFs(
+  content: string | null
+): FocusFs & {
+  setContent: (nextContent: string | null) => void;
+  triggerChange: () => void;
+  triggerError: (err: unknown) => void;
+  close: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
+} {
+  let currentContent = content;
+  let onChange: (() => void) | undefined;
+  let onError: ((err: unknown) => void) | undefined;
+  const close = vi.fn();
+  return {
+    readFile: async () => currentContent,
+    watchDir: (_dir, onC, onE) => {
+      onChange = onC;
+      onError = onE;
+      return { close };
+    },
+    setContent: nextContent => {
+      currentContent = nextContent;
+    },
+    triggerChange: () => onChange?.(),
+    triggerError: err => onError?.(err),
+    close,
+  };
+}

+        nodeId,
+      })
+    );
+    fs.triggerChange();
+    await flushFocusReads();
+
+    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledWith(request('request-2'));
+    watcher.dispose();
+  });
+

+    const fs = mockFs(null);
+    const onRequest = vi.fn();
+    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
+      dir: '/workspace',
+      filename: 'focus.json',
+      fs,
+      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
+      onRequest,

diff --git a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
index c2c5baf4229ed18429a4722e2e4b5a73e4b99142..e7000de4e464593331db2561c4a5edde1cf1877e
--- a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
@@ -125,3 +125,143 @@
     return this.queue.length;
   }
 }
+
+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+function mockFs(
+  content: string | null
+): FocusFs & {
+  setContent: (nextContent: string | null) => void;
+  triggerChange: () => void;
+  triggerError: (err: unknown) => void;
+  close: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
+} {
+  let currentContent = content;
+  let onChange: (() => void) | undefined;
+  let onError: ((err: unknown) => void) | undefined;
+  const close = vi.fn();
+  return {
+    readFile: async () => currentContent,
+    watchDir: (_dir, onC, onE) => {
+      onChange = onC;
+      onError = onE;
+      return { close };
+    },
+    setContent: nextContent => {
+      currentContent = nextContent;
+    },
+    triggerChange: () => onChange?.(),
+    triggerError: err => onError?.(err),
+    close,
+  };
+}
+

+  await Promise.resolve();
+  await Promise.resolve();
+}

diff --git a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
index c2c5baf4229ed18429a4722e2e4b5a73e4b99142..e7000de4e464593331db2561c4a5edde1cf1877e
--- a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
@@ -125,3 +125,143 @@
     return this.queue.length;
   }
 }
+
+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}

+  dir: string;

+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;

+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions

+    } catch {

+      return;
+    }
+

+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }

+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }

+    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
+      dir: '/workspace',
+      filename: 'focus.json',
+      fs,
+      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
+      onRequest,
+    });
+    await flushFocusReads();
+    fs.setContent(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        workspace: 'workspace-a',
+        requestId: 'request-3',
+        nodeId,
+      })
+    );
+
+    fs.triggerChange();
+    await flushFocusReads();
+    fs.triggerChange();
+    await flushFocusReads();

+    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();

diff --git a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
index c2c5baf4229ed18429a4722e2e4b5a73e4b99142..e7000de4e464593331db2561c4a5edde1cf1877e
--- a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
@@ -125,3 +125,143 @@
     return this.queue.length;
   }
 }
+
+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+function mockFs(
+  content: string | null
+): FocusFs & {
+  setContent: (nextContent: string | null) => void;
+  triggerChange: () => void;
+  triggerError: (err: unknown) => void;
+  close: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
+} {
+  let currentContent = content;
+  let onChange: (() => void) | undefined;
+  let onError: ((err: unknown) => void) | undefined;
+  const close = vi.fn();
+  return {
+    readFile: async () => currentContent,
+    watchDir: (_dir, onC, onE) => {
+      onChange = onC;
+      onError = onE;
+      return { close };
+    },
+    setContent: nextContent => {
+      currentContent = nextContent;
+    },
+    triggerChange: () => onChange?.(),
+    triggerError: err => onError?.(err),
+    close,
+  };
+}
+
+async function flushFocusReads(): Promise<void> {
+  await Promise.resolve();
+  await Promise.resolve();

diff --git a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
index c2c5baf4229ed18429a4722e2e4b5a73e4b99142..e7000de4e464593331db2561c4a5edde1cf1877e
--- a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
@@ -125,3 +125,143 @@
     return this.queue.length;
   }
 }
+
+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

diff --git a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
index c2c5baf4229ed18429a4722e2e4b5a73e4b99142..e7000de4e464593331db2561c4a5edde1cf1877e
--- a/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
+++ b/packages/foam-vscode/src/pa-hub/focus.ts
@@ -125,3 +125,143 @@
     return this.queue.length;
   }
 }
+
+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

 const nodeId = 'vault:test-node' as NodeId;
 
@@ -8,6 +13,39 @@
   return { requestId, nodeId };
 }
 
+function mockFs(
+  content: string | null
+): FocusFs & {
+  setContent: (nextContent: string | null) => void;
+  triggerChange: () => void;
+  triggerError: (err: unknown) => void;
+  close: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
+} {
+  let currentContent = content;
+  let onChange: (() => void) | undefined;
+  let onError: ((err: unknown) => void) | undefined;
+  const close = vi.fn();
+  return {
+    readFile: async () => currentContent,
+    watchDir: (_dir, onC, onE) => {
+      onChange = onC;
+      onError = onE;
+      return { close };
+    },
+    setContent: nextContent => {
+      currentContent = nextContent;
+    },
+    triggerChange: () => onChange?.(),
+    triggerError: err => onError?.(err),
+    close,
+  };
+}
+
+async function flushFocusReads(): Promise<void> {
+  await Promise.resolve();
+  await Promise.resolve();
+}
+
 describe('focus', () => {
   it('test_focus_parse_valid', () => {
     expect(
@@ -132,4 +170,97 @@
     queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
     expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([]);
   });
+
+  it('test_focus_watch_startup_suppress', async () => {
+    const fs = mockFs(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        workspace: 'workspace-a',
+        requestId: 'request-1',
+        nodeId,
+      })
+    );
+    const onRequest = vi.fn();
+    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
+      dir: '/workspace',
+      filename: 'focus.json',
+      fs,
+      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
+      onRequest,
+    });
+
+    await flushFocusReads();
+
+    expect(onRequest).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    watcher.dispose();
+  });
+
+  it('test_focus_watch_new_request', async () => {
+    const fs = mockFs(null);
+    const onRequest = vi.fn();
+    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
+      dir: '/workspace',
+      filename: 'focus.json',
+      fs,
+      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
+      onRequest,
+    });
+    await flushFocusReads();
+
+    fs.setContent(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        workspace: 'workspace-a',
+        requestId: 'request-2',
+        nodeId,
+      })
+    );
+    fs.triggerChange();
+    await flushFocusReads();
+
+    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledWith(request('request-2'));
+    watcher.dispose();
+  });
+
+  it('test_focus_watch_duplicate_suppress', async () => {
+    const fs = mockFs(null);
+    const onRequest = vi.fn();
+    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
+      dir: '/workspace',
+      filename: 'focus.json',
+      fs,
+      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
+      onRequest,
+    });
+    await flushFocusReads();
+    fs.setContent(
+      JSON.stringify({
+        workspace: 'workspace-a',
+        requestId: 'request-3',
+        nodeId,
+      })
+    );
+
+    fs.triggerChange();
+    await flushFocusReads();
+    fs.triggerChange();
+    await flushFocusReads();
+
+    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+    watcher.dispose();
+  });
+
+  it('test_focus_watch_dispose', () => {
+    const fs = mockFs(null);
+    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
+      dir: '/workspace',
+      filename: 'focus.json',
+      fs,
+      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
+      onRequest: vi.fn(),
+    });
+
+    watcher.dispose();
+
+    expect(fs.close).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+  });
 });

+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+
+export interface FocusFs {
+  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
+  watchDir(
+    dir: string,
+    onChange: () => void,
+    onError: (err: unknown) => void
+  ): { close(): void };
+}
+
+export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
+  dir: string;
+  filename: string;
+  fs: FocusFs;
+  workspaceId: string;
+  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
+}
+
+const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;
+
+export function watchFocusFile(
+  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
+): { dispose(): void } {
+  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
+  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+  let disposed = false;
+  let reading = false;
+
+  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
+    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
+    if (content === null) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+    });

+    await flushFocusReads();

+    expect(onRequest).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+    watcher.dispose();

 succeeded in 0ms:
      this.queue = [this.queue[this.queue.length - 1]];
    }
  }

  drain(): FocusRequest[] {
    const drained = this.queue;
    this.queue = [];
    for (const request of drained) {
      this.markSeen(request.requestId);
    }
    return drained;
  }

  markSeen(requestId: string): void {
    if (this.seen.includes(requestId)) {
      return;
    }

    this.seen.push(requestId);
    if (this.seen.length > this.maxSeen) {
      this.seen = this.seen.slice(Math.floor(this.seen.length / 2));
    }
  }

  get pending(): number {
    return this.queue.length;
  }
}

export interface FocusFs {
  readFile(path: string): Promise<string | null>;
  watchDir(
    dir: string,
    onChange: () => void,
    onError: (err: unknown) => void
  ): { close(): void };
}

export interface WatchFocusFileOptions {
  dir: string;
  filename: string;
  fs: FocusFs;
  workspaceId: string;
  onRequest: (request: FocusRequest) => void;
}

const focusWatchReattachDelayMs = 1000;

export function watchFocusFile(
  options: WatchFocusFileOptions
): { dispose(): void } {
  const filePath = `${options.dir}/${options.filename}`;
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  let watcher: { close(): void } | undefined;
  let reattachTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  let disposed = false;
  let reading = false;

  async function readRequest(startup: boolean): Promise<void> {
    const content = await options.fs.readFile(filePath);
    if (content === null) {
      return;
    }

    let parsed: unknown;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
    } catch {
      return;
    }

    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
    });
    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
      return;
    }

    const { request } = result;
    if (startup) {
      seen.add(request.requestId);
      return;
    }

    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
      return;
    }

    seen.add(request.requestId);
    options.onRequest(request);
  }

  function readOnChange(): void {
    if (disposed || reading) {
      return;
    }

    reading = true;
    void readRequest(false)
      .catch(() => undefined)
      .finally(() => {
        reading = false;
      });
  }

  function scheduleReattach(): void {
    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
      return;
    }

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      reattachTimer = undefined;
      attachWatcher();
    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
    reattachTimer = timer;

    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
    unref?.call(timer);
  }

  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
    if (disposed) {
      return;
    }

    watcher?.close();
    watcher = undefined;
    scheduleReattach();
  }

  function attachWatcher(): void {
    if (disposed) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
        options.dir,
        readOnChange,
        handleWatcherError
      );
    } catch (error) {
      handleWatcherError(error);
    }
  }

  attachWatcher();

  reading = true;
  void readRequest(true)
    .catch(() => undefined)
    .finally(() => {
      reading = false;
    });

  return {
    dispose(): void {
      disposed = true;
      watcher?.close();
      watcher = undefined;
      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
        reattachTimer = undefined;
      }
    },
  };
}
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with { 'resolution-mode': 'import' };
import {
  FocusQueue,
  parseFocusFilePayload,
  watchFocusFile,
} from '../focus';
import type { FocusFs } from '../focus';

const nodeId = 'vault:test-node' as NodeId;

function request(requestId: string) {
  return { requestId, nodeId };
}

function mockFs(
  content: string | null
): FocusFs & {
  setContent: (nextContent: string | null) => void;
  triggerChange: () => void;
  triggerError: (err: unknown) => void;
  close: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
} {
  let currentContent = content;
  let onChange: (() => void) | undefined;
  let onError: ((err: unknown) => void) | undefined;
  const close = vi.fn();
  return {
    readFile: async () => currentContent,
    watchDir: (_dir, onC, onE) => {
      onChange = onC;
      onError = onE;
      return { close };
    },
    setContent: nextContent => {
      currentContent = nextContent;
    },
    triggerChange: () => onChange?.(),
    triggerError: err => onError?.(err),
    close,
  };
}

async function flushFocusReads(): Promise<void> {
  await Promise.resolve();
  await Promise.resolve();
}

describe('focus', () => {
  it('test_focus_parse_valid', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      request: { requestId: 'request-1', nodeId },
    });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_valid_center', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        {
          workspace: 'workspace-a',
          requestId: 'request-1',
          nodeId,
          center: true,
        },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      request: { requestId: 'request-1', nodeId, center: true },
    });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_invalid_shape', () => {
    for (const input of [
      null,
      42,
      {},
      { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1' },
      { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId: 42 },
    ]) {
      expect(
        parseFocusFilePayload(input, { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' })
      ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'invalid_shape' });
    }
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_wrong_workspace', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-b', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'wrong_workspace' });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_missing_request_id', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: '', nodeId },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'missing_request_id' });
  });

  it('test_focus_parse_invalid_node_id', () => {
    expect(
      parseFocusFilePayload(
        { workspace: 'workspace-a', requestId: 'request-1', nodeId: 'bad' },
        { workspaceId: 'workspace-a' }
      )
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'invalid_node_id' });
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_replay_suppression', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue({ requestId: 'request-1', nodeId: 'vault:other-node' as NodeId });

    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-1')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_keep_latest_not_ready', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue(request('request-2'));

    expect(queue.pending).toBe(1);
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-2')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_accumulate_ready', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.markReady();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue(request('request-2'));

    expect(queue.pending).toBe(2);
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-1'), request('request-2')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_mark_not_ready_keeps_latest', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.markReady();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    queue.enqueue(request('request-2'));
    queue.markNotReady();

    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-2')]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_mark_seen_suppresses', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.markSeen('request-1');
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));

    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('test_focus_queue_drain_adds_to_seen', () => {
    const queue = new FocusQueue();
    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([request('request-1')]);

    queue.enqueue(request('request-1'));
    expect(queue.drain()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('test_focus_watch_startup_suppress', async () => {
    const fs = mockFs(
      JSON.stringify({
        workspace: 'workspace-a',
        requestId: 'request-1',
        nodeId,
      })
    );
    const onRequest = vi.fn();
    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
      dir: '/workspace',
      filename: 'focus.json',
      fs,
      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
      onRequest,
    });

    await flushFocusReads();

    expect(onRequest).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    watcher.dispose();
  });

  it('test_focus_watch_new_request', async () => {
    const fs = mockFs(null);
    const onRequest = vi.fn();
    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
      dir: '/workspace',
      filename: 'focus.json',
      fs,
      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
      onRequest,
    });
    await flushFocusReads();

    fs.setContent(
      JSON.stringify({
        workspace: 'workspace-a',
        requestId: 'request-2',
        nodeId,
      })
    );
    fs.triggerChange();
    await flushFocusReads();

    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledWith(request('request-2'));
    watcher.dispose();
  });

  it('test_focus_watch_duplicate_suppress', async () => {
    const fs = mockFs(null);
    const onRequest = vi.fn();
    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
      dir: '/workspace',
      filename: 'focus.json',
      fs,
      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
      onRequest,
    });
    await flushFocusReads();
    fs.setContent(
      JSON.stringify({
        workspace: 'workspace-a',
        requestId: 'request-3',
        nodeId,
      })
    );

    fs.triggerChange();
    await flushFocusReads();
    fs.triggerChange();
    await flushFocusReads();

    expect(onRequest).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    watcher.dispose();
  });

  it('test_focus_watch_dispose', () => {
    const fs = mockFs(null);
    const watcher = watchFocusFile({
      dir: '/workspace',
      filename: 'focus.json',
      fs,
      workspaceId: 'workspace-a',
      onRequest: vi.fn(),
    });

    watcher.dispose();

    expect(fs.close).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});

+    }
+
+    let parsed: unknown;
+    try {
+      parsed = JSON.parse(content);
+    } catch {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const result = parseFocusFilePayload(parsed, {
+      workspaceId: options.workspaceId,
+    });
+    if (!result.ok || disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const { request } = result;
+    if (startup) {
+      seen.add(request.requestId);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (seen.has(request.requestId)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    seen.add(request.requestId);
+    options.onRequest(request);
+  }
+
+  function readOnChange(): void {
+    if (disposed || reading) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    reading = true;
+    void readRequest(false)
+      .catch(() => undefined)
+      .finally(() => {
+        reading = false;
+      });
+  }
+
+  function scheduleReattach(): void {
+    if (disposed || reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      reattachTimer = undefined;
+      attachWatcher();
+    }, focusWatchReattachDelayMs);
+    reattachTimer = timer;
+
+    // Unref Node timers when available; the VS Code layer owns watcher disposal.
+    const unref = (timer as unknown as { unref?: () => void }).unref;
+    unref?.call(timer);
+  }
+
+  function handleWatcherError(_error: unknown): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    watcher?.close();
+    watcher = undefined;
+    scheduleReattach();
+  }
+
+  function attachWatcher(): void {
+    if (disposed) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      watcher = options.fs.watchDir(
+        options.dir,
+        readOnChange,
+        handleWatcherError
+      );
+    } catch (error) {
+      handleWatcherError(error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  attachWatcher();
+
+  reading = true;
+  void readRequest(true)
+    .catch(() => undefined)
+    .finally(() => {
+      reading = false;
+    });
+
+  return {
+    dispose(): void {
+      disposed = true;
+      watcher?.close();
+      watcher = undefined;
+      if (reattachTimer !== undefined) {
+        clearTimeout(reattachTimer);
+        reattachTimer = undefined;
+      }
+    },
+  };
+}

+function mockFs(
+  content: string | null
+): FocusFs & {
+  setContent: (nextContent: string | null) => void;
+  triggerChange: () => void;
+  triggerError: (err: unknown) => void;
+  close: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
+} {
+  let currentContent = content;
+  let onChange: (() => void) | undefined;
+  let onError: ((err: unknown) => void) | undefined;
+  const close = vi.fn();
+  return {
+    readFile: async () => currentContent,
+    watchDir: (_dir, onC, onE) => {
+      onChange = onC;
+      onError = onE;
+      return { close };
+    },
+    setContent: nextContent => {
+      currentContent = nextContent;
+    },
+    triggerChange: () => onChange?.(),
+    triggerError: err => onError?.(err),
+    close,
+  };
+}

+  await Promise.resolve();
